# Integration implementation register

> Generated from integrations/register.json, integrations/history.jsonl and the data contracts. Do not edit this view by hand.

**22 sources/internal producers · 127 capability rows · 0 independently approved.**

Prepared on 2026-10-31T11:30:02Z by Codex assistant (repository preparation). Progress is derived from the recorded work history.

This is the central work list. Planned support is not a working connection. An approval applies only to the explicit field subset, source revision and test environment recorded below. Partial coverage never becomes a complete business answer.

[How to use and update this register](integrations/README.md) · [Append-only work history](integrations/history.jsonl) · [Machine-readable plan](integrations/register.json)

[Historical import and data readiness contract](specs/IMPORT-ONBOARDING-AND-DATA-READINESS.md): date selection, older open/related items, opening state, truthful progress, source health and per-answer readiness. Every capability must provide its own mapping and test evidence. This development register is not a tenant runtime health dashboard.

The five tracks are Source, Fact, Commitment, Movement and Reservation. They are logical journal tracks; physical SQL tables follow the journal contract. Each event uses only the applicable tracks.

**Active scope: local pilot.** [delivery-profile.json](delivery-profile.json) selects the required capability rows. Scope labels below preserve the broader platform plan; they do not make every first_release row a pilot requirement. Use the [local pilot contract](specs/LOCAL-PILOT.md) and its release gate. Deferred providers remain planned and unverified.

## Per-source work list

Track initials: **S** Source · **F** Fact · **C** Commitment · **M** Movement · **R** Reservation. “—” means no event in this data contract requires that track. Step counts are work claims, not test passes. Tests show the four required suites. Live evidence is separate.

<a id="source-shopify"></a>
### Shopify

**Pilot:** required. **Platform plan:** first_release. **Documentation:** reviewed.

**Input:** GraphQL Admin API: paginated backfill and reconciliation; verified HTTPS webhooks trigger durable receipt and re-fetch. Pin an API version and fixture schema; consume all nested pages.

**Prerequisites:** read_orders; older order access needs read_all_orders approval. read_inventory and appropriate fulfillment-order scopes; read_returns for return objects. Prove actual grants and protected customer-data access. Record history start and every missing scope.

#### Synchronization by capability

Mode plans are investigation obligations. Only committed implementation mappings declare support, and test/approval columns above remain the verification authority. Local standalone has no public webhook callback. Polling may recover current state without recovering every intermediate event.

| Capability | Local standalone | Local + gateway | Public backend |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| shopify.orders | supported: polling; schedule: Incremental poll every 300 s (bounds 60-3600 s, jitter 30 s); reconciliation every 6 h over a 48 h window; backfill paged with checkpoints.; history: Default order access is the last 60 days; older orders require the read_all_orders approval. Only the current version of an order is observable; edits before the first poll are not reconstructed.; limits: No webhooks: changes are seen at the next poll. Nested connections beyond the selected page sizes are flagged truncated_connections (lineItems are completed by follow-up pages). Only the current revision of an order is readable; line removals and refunds are distinct (currentQuantity vs refund transactions).; reason: Outbound GraphQL polling needs no inbound route and is supported in local_standalone. | unsupported: none; schedule: none; history: none; limits: No gateway runtime exists in the pilot; declared and refused by the connector (LOCAL-07).; reason: local_gateway is deferred scope for the local pilot (delivery-profile.json); no gateway is provisioned and the capability declaration lists no methods for it. | unsupported: none; schedule: none; history: none; limits: No public callback is registered; localhost cannot receive Shopify webhooks.; reason: public_server is deferred scope for the local pilot; direct webhooks would require a public HTTPS endpoint that the pilot does not provide. |
| shopify.stock | partial: polling; schedule: Poll every 900 s (300 s-6 h, jitter 60 s); reconciliation daily.; history: No stock history: only current levels (snapshot_only).; limits: Snapshot-only; named states depend on location/app configuration; levels beyond 10 locations per item are flagged.; reason: Current levels are readable by polling; movement history is not, so the capability stays partial. | unsupported: none; schedule: none; history: none; limits: No gateway runtime exists in the pilot; declared and refused by the connector (LOCAL-07).; reason: local_gateway is deferred scope for the local pilot (delivery-profile.json); no gateway is provisioned and the capability declaration lists no methods for it. | unsupported: none; schedule: none; history: none; limits: No public callback is registered; localhost cannot receive Shopify webhooks.; reason: public_server is deferred scope for the local pilot; direct webhooks would require a public HTTPS endpoint that the pilot does not provide. |

<details><summary>Historical import and data readiness obligations</summary>

| Capability | Investigation / actual mapping |
| --- | --- |
| shopify.orders | time_selection: Order.createdAt selects the import window (business occurrence); Order.updatedAt selects incremental/reconciliation reads (last modification). Observation time is the poll time; recorded_at is the actual journal commit time and is never backdated.; historical_availability: Default order access covers the last 60 days; older orders need the read_all_orders approval, which is recorded as a restriction on the plan and shown as an accessible-period limit. The current order version is observable, not its intermediate edits: history_kind event_history for new activity, snapshot for the state at first read.; open_and_related_records: With include_open_items the "open" backfill phase retrieves orders created before the window that are still open/unpaid/unshipped; their transactions, fulfillments, refunds and returns come with the order. Traversal is bounded to the order and its nested objects; no unrelated customer history is crawled.; opening_state: No opening balance applies to orders; the first read of an older open order is a snapshot of its current state labeled observation_mode=backfill, never a new sale.; progress_accounting: requested = exact ordersCount of the window (precision EXACT) plus open phase; loaded = orders fetched; committed = distinct logical records with a new journal entry; duplicates/conflicts/rejected are separate; totals_known is true only for backfills with exact counts. Percentages are only shown for the backfill fetch stage.; readiness_rules: Order questions are ready when the connection has a completed backfill for the declared window and the incremental cursor is current within the schedule; partial when the open phase or line-item follow-ups are incomplete (truncated_connections) or the 60-day limit cuts the requested window; blocked without read_orders.; health_rules: healthy after a complete successful poll within interval + jitter; degraded after a failed/partial poll, missing grant or conflicts; unavailable on provider 5xx/429 beyond retries; paused on storage critical. next_check_at is null with a reason (no_import_plan, missing_grant, storage_critical).; history_expansion: A new plan revision creates a history_expansion/backfill run over the additional interval; overlaps are deduplicated by provider_event_id; recorded_at of new entries is the actual import time; prior coverage is preserved.; data_handling: Only opaque gids, amounts, quantities, statuses and timestamps enter payloads; customer is referenced by gid only; evidence stores the sanitized provider object without names, emails or addresses. |
| shopify.stock | time_selection: InventoryLevel.updatedAt is the snapshot time; a date plan does not apply to stock (there is no stock history) and the plan window is disclosed as not applicable for this capability.; historical_availability: Only the current state is available (history_kind snapshot_only); no earlier levels are reconstructed.; open_and_related_records: All items/locations are read; related orders are not traversed from stock.; opening_state: The first snapshot is the opening stock as of its updatedAt; it is a fact, not a movement.; progress_accounting: loaded = inventory items fetched; committed = distinct (item, location) records with a new snapshot; totals unknown (no provider count) so counts and phase are shown, no percentage.; readiness_rules: Stock questions are ready for items with a snapshot not older than the schedule; partial for items without named states (null); free stock derivations require physical/committed/reserved/damaged/safety_stock/quality_control to be present.; health_rules: healthy after a complete poll within interval + jitter; degraded on missing read_inventory; stale snapshots are reported with their observed_at.; history_expansion: Not applicable beyond re-reading current levels; a history expansion creates no older snapshots.; data_handling: Only gids, SKU strings and integer quantities; no personal data. |
| shopify.allocations | time_selection: FulfillmentOrder.updatedAt selects both the import window and incremental reads (no created_at filter is used).; historical_availability: Current assignments only; earlier assignment changes are not reconstructed.; open_and_related_records: Open fulfillment orders of older orders are included through the updated_at window only; related orders are read by shopify.orders.; opening_state: Not applicable: an assignment is not an opening reservation balance.; progress_accounting: loaded = fulfillment orders fetched; committed = distinct fulfillment orders with a new fact; totals unknown.; readiness_rules: Allocation questions that need reservations are blocked/partial for Shopify-only workspaces (explicit missing fields); questions about outstanding fulfillment work are ready after a complete poll.; health_rules: healthy after a complete poll within interval + jitter; degraded on missing fulfillment-order scopes.; history_expansion: Re-reading a wider updated_at window; no older reservations appear.; data_handling: Only gids and quantities. |

Percentages are only shown for the backfill fetch stage.; readiness_rules: Order questions are ready when the connection has a completed backfill for the declared window and the incremental cursor is current within the schedule; partial when the open phase or line-item follow-ups are incomplete (truncated_connections) or the 60-day limit cuts the requested window; blocked without read_orders.; health_rules: healthy after a complete successful poll within interval + jitter; degraded after a failed/partial poll, missing grant or conflicts; unavailable on provider 5xx/429 beyond retries; paused on storage critical. next_check_at is null with a reason (no_import_plan, missing_grant, storage_critical).; history_expansion: A new plan revision creates a history_expansion/backfill run over the additional interval; overlaps are deduplicated by provider_event_id; recorded_at of new entries is the actual import time; prior coverage is preserved.; data_handling: Only opaque gids, amounts, quantities, statuses and timestamps enter payloads; customer is referenced by gid only; evidence stores the sanitized provider object without names, emails or addresses. |
| shopify.refunds | time_selection: Order.createdAt selects the import window (business occurrence); Order.updatedAt selects incremental/reconciliation reads (last modification). Observation time is the poll time; recorded_at is the actual journal commit time and is never backdated.; historical_availability: Default order access covers the last 60 days; older orders need the read_all_orders approval, which is recorded as a restriction on the plan and shown as an accessible-period limit. The current order version is observable, not its intermediate edits: history_kind event_history for new activity, snapshot for the state at first read.; open_and_related_records: With include_open_items the "open" backfill phase retrieves orders created before the window that are still open/unpaid/unshipped; their transactions, fulfillments, refunds and returns come with the order. Traversal is bounded to the order and its nested objects; no unrelated customer history is crawled.; opening_state: No opening balance applies to orders; the first read of an older open order is a snapshot of its current state labeled observation_mode=backfill, never a new sale.; progress_accounting: requested = exact ordersCount of the window (precision EXACT) plus open phase; loaded = orders fetched; committed = distinct logical records with a new journal entry; duplicates/conflicts/rejected are separate; totals_known is true only for backfills with exact counts. Percentages are only shown for the backfill fetch stage.; readiness_rules: Order questions are ready when the connection has a completed backfill for the declared window and the incremental cursor is current within the schedule; partial when the open phase or line-item follow-ups are incomplete (truncated_connections) or the 60-day limit cuts the requested window; blocked without read_orders.; health_rules: healthy after a complete successful poll within interval + jitter; degraded after a failed/partial poll, missing grant or conflicts; unavailable on provider 5xx/429 beyond retries; paused on storage critical. next_check_at is null with a reason (no_import_plan, missing_grant, storage_critical).; history_expansion: A new plan revision creates a history_expansion/backfill run over the additional interval; overlaps are deduplicated by provider_event_id; recorded_at of new entries is the actual import time; prior coverage is preserved.; data_handling: Only opaque gids, amounts, quantities, statuses and timestamps enter payloads; customer is referenced by gid only; evidence stores the sanitized provider object without names, emails or addresses. |

rust: crates/domain reducers (effect-once per observation, duplicates/conflicts/late entries, corrections via supersedes_entry_id), crates/projections tail/checkpoint/snapshot/rebuild, bounded RAM with cold reads; verified by cargo test --workspace --release (golden checkpoints, adversarial variants, 1000-entry tail, corrupt snapshot rebuild).; query_mcp: crates/query intents + apps/api question/state routes + packages/mcp stdio bridge: identical normalized answers through API and MCP (SLICE-01 step 4/5), explicit partial/unknown/conflict with missing_inputs, committed/applied cursors and as_of cutoffs.; operations: capability_health (last successful check vs last new event, next check + reason, coverage), import_runs counters, worker dead letters, sanitized structured logs (no tokens/payloads), fences for backup/restore/upgrade; | 2026-11-02T02:30:33Z / Claude Fable 5.1 (P4 re-run verifier) | shopify.payments / implementation |   Re-records the implementation of row shopify.payments on the committed revision df8cfbb5e41fb3da483270651823cf470aaf1a65 (P4 re-run verifier). The business mappings, adapters, reducers and query services are unchanged since the invalidated revision 482bdf5; the revision carries the engine health-latch fix in crates/projections/src/engine.rs and the MCP get_evidence description naming the explicit `restricted` status. Synthetic provider-shaped fixture only; live access remains a separate blocked event. Known limitations unchanged: Pending SALE transactions unsupported; fees come from the Shopify Payments ledger, not from the order.; mapping: Committed mapping artifact integrations/mappings/shopify.payments.json (pinned GraphQL Admin API 2026-10, exact source paths, missing fields explicit, three mode declarations, nine import-readiness rules, event rules per family); validated by integrations/mappings/validate-mappings.py.; transport: packages/connectors/src/adapters/shopify: Order.transactions nested in the orders group (AUTHORIZATION/CAPTURE/SALE/VOID with explicit test flag); client credentials or admin token (never logged), calculated-cost throttling, 429 Retry-After, 5xx backoff, expired cursors, account identity check (reauthorization_required on mismatch), gateway/public modes declared unsupported and refused.; postgres: apps/worker/src/sync.ts + packages/connectors/src/ingest.ts: deliveries dedupe boundary, journal_evidence, append_journal_entries(jsonb) as bb_worker under RLS, derived entries with input_entry_ids/derivation_rule, checkpoint after every COMMIT, conflicts as source.conflict_observed + review case, dead letters for rejected input.; rust: crates/domain reducers (effect-once per observation, duplicates/conflicts/late entries, corrections via supersedes_entry_id), crates/projections tail/checkpoint/snapshot/rebuild, bounded RAM with cold reads; verified by cargo test --workspace --release (golden checkpoints, adversarial variants, 1000-entry tail, corrupt snapshot rebuild).; query_mcp: crates/query intents + apps/api question/state routes + packages/mcp stdio bridge: identical normalized answers through API and MCP (SLICE-01 step 4/5), explicit partial/unknown/conflict with missing_inputs, committed/applied cursors and as_of cutoffs.; operations: capability_health (last successful check vs last new event, next check + reason, coverage), import_runs counters, worker dead letters, sanitized structured logs (no tokens/payloads), fences for backup/restore/upgrade; runbook in docs/connectors.md and docs/operations.md. | [integrations/mappings/shopify.payments.json](integrations/mappings/shopify.payments.json); df8cfbb5e41fb3da483270651823cf470aaf1a65 |

<a id="source-shopify_payments"></a>
### Shopify Payments

**Pilot:** required. **Platform plan:** first_release. **Documentation:** reviewed.

**Input:** Query ShopifyPaymentsAccount balanceTransactions, payouts and disputes with pagination; reconcile provider balances independently of order ingestion.

**Prerequisites:** An eligible Shopify Payments account and actual finance/dispute grants. Account docs list read_shopify_payments or read_shopify_payments_accounts; payout objects require view_payouts permission and disputes require read_shopify_payments_disputes. Validate all field-level access in the pinned schema/account.

#### Synchronization by capability

Mode plans are investigation obligations. Only committed implementation mappings declare support, and test/approval columns above remain the verification authority. Local standalone has no public webhook callback. Polling may recover current state without recovering every intermediate event.

| Capability | Local standalone | Local + gateway | Public backend |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| shopify_payments.provider_ledger | partial: polling; schedule: Poll every 1800 s (bounds 300 s-24 h, jitter 120 s); reconciliation every 24 h over 7 days.; history: Account history as exposed by the pinned API; the exact retention of balance transactions on the account is not verifiable offline and is recorded from the live account at SLICE-02/SOURCE-01 time.; limits: Whether the balanceTransactions connection accepts a processed_at query filter was not verifiable offline (the fixture implements it); if the live API rejects it the adapter falls back to full pagination with client-side filtering, recorded as a limitation at SOURCE-01 time.; reason: Outbound polling of the Shopify Payments account works for the declared subset; missing fields keep the capability partial. | unsupported: none; schedule: none; history: none; limits: No gateway runtime exists in the pilot; declared and refused by the connector (LOCAL-07).; reason: local_gateway is deferred scope for the local pilot (delivery-profile.json); no gateway is provisioned and the capability declaration lists no methods for it. | unsupported: none; schedule: none; history: none; limits: No public callback is registered; localhost cannot receive Shopify webhooks.; reason: public_server is deferred scope for the local pilot; direct webhooks would require a public HTTPS endpoint that the pilot does not provide. |
| shopify_payments.positions | partial: polling; schedule: Poll every 1800 s (bounds 300 s-24 h, jitter 120 s); reconciliation every 24 h over 7 days.; history: Account history as exposed by the pinned API; the exact retention of balance transactions on the account is not verifiable offline and is recorded from the live account at SLICE-02/SOURCE-01 time.; limits: Only the total balance is exposed.; reason: Outbound polling of the Shopify Payments account works for the declared subset; missing fields keep the capability partial. | unsupported: none; schedule: none; history: none; limits: No gateway runtime exists in the pilot; declared and refused by the connector (LOCAL-07).; reason: local_gateway is deferred scope for the local pilot (delivery-profile.json); no gateway is provisioned and the capability declaration lists no methods for it. | unsupported: none; schedule: none; history: none; limits: No public callback is registered; localhost cannot receive Shopify webhooks.; reason: public_server is deferred scope for the local pilot; direct webhooks would require a public HTTPS endpoint that the pilot does not provide. |

<details><summary>Historical import and data readiness obligations</summary>

| Capability | Investigation / actual mapping |
| --- | --- |
| shopify_payments.provider_ledger | time_selection: transactionDate (processed_at) selects records; payout membership is an attribute, not a time filter.; historical_availability: Balance transactions and payouts as far back as the account exposes them; the exact depth is not verifiable offline and is recorded from the authorized account (blocked_external until then).; open_and_related_records: Entries linked to orders outside the plan window are kept (their order link is an optional attribute); no order traversal from the ledger.; opening_state: Account balance snapshots (positions) are the opening state as of the poll time; they are never summed with ledger movements.; progress_accounting: loaded = balance transactions fetched; committed = distinct ledger entries with new journal entries; totals unknown.; readiness_rules: Provider ledger questions are ready after a complete backfill and a current poll; partial when sourceOrderTransactionId links are absent for charges in scope.; health_rules: healthy after a complete poll within 1800 s + jitter; degraded on missing read_shopify_payments_payouts or inactive account.; history_expansion: Wider processed_at window; duplicates deduplicated by id.; data_handling: Only gids, amounts, types, statuses and timestamps; no bank account numbers (bankAccounts is not queried). |
| shopify_payments.positions | time_selection: Poll time (no as-of time is published).; historical_availability: Balance transactions and payouts as far back as the account exposes them; the exact depth is not verifiable offline and is recorded from the authorized account (blocked_external until then).; open_and_related_records: Not applicable.; opening_state: Account balance snapshots (positions) are the opening state as of the poll time; they are never summed with ledger movements.; progress_accounting: loaded = currencies read; committed = snapshots; totals known (number of currencies).; readiness_rules: Provider position questions are partial by construction (components unknown); the total is ready after the first snapshot.; health_rules: healthy after a snapshot within 1800 s + jitter.; history_expansion: Not applicable.; data_handling: Only gids, amounts, types, statuses and timestamps; no bank account numbers (bankAccounts is not queried). |
| shopify_payments.payouts | time_selection: issuedAt selects payouts.; historical_availability: Balance transactions and payouts as far back as the account exposes them; the exact depth is not verifiable offline and is recorded from the authorized account (blocked_external until then).; open_and_related_records: Unsettled payouts (SCHEDULED/IN_TRANSIT) before the window are included through the open-items phase of the plan.; opening_state: Account balance snapshots (positions) are the opening state as of the poll time; they are never summed with ledger movements.; progress_accounting: loaded = payouts fetched; committed = distinct payouts with new observations; totals unknown.; readiness_rules: Payout questions are ready after a complete backfill and current poll; bank-settlement answers stay partial until bank_csv evidence matches.; health_rules: healthy after a complete poll; degraded on missing scope/inactive account.; history_expansion: Wider issued_at window; status transitions already observed are duplicates.; data_handling: Only gids, amounts, types, statuses and timestamps; no bank account numbers (bankAccounts is not queried). |
| shopify_payments.holds | time_selection: Not applicable (no resource).; historical_availability: Not available.; open_and_related_records: Not applicable.; opening_state: Not applicable; held balance components are unknown.; progress_accounting: No run is created; the capability shows unsupported instead of counters.; readiness_rules: Questions requiring holds are blocked/unknown for Shopify-only workspaces and say so.; health_rules: Health shows unsupported with the reason; no stale/overdue state is derived for it.; history_expansion: Not applicable.; data_handling: No data is read. |
| shopify_payments.refunds | time_selection: transactionDate of the REFUND posting.; historical_availability: Balance transactions and payouts as far back as the account exposes them; the exact depth is not verifiable offline and is recorded from the authorized account (blocked_external until then).; open_and_related_records: The refunded order is an optional link; no traversal.; opening_state: Account balance snapshots (positions) are the opening state as of the poll time; they are never summed with ledger movements.; progress_accounting: Counted within the ledger poll (same provider query).; readiness_rules: Refund cash questions are ready when both shop and ledger refunds are current; partial when ledger postings lack sourceOrderTransactionId.; health_rules: Shared with provider_ledger.; history_expansion: Shared with provider_ledger.; data_handling: Only gids, amounts, types, statuses and timestamps; no bank account numbers (bankAccounts is not queried). |
| shopify_payments.disputes | time_selection: initiatedAt selects dispute cases; finalizedOn is the resolution time; postings use transactionDate.; historical_availability: Balance transactions and payouts as far back as the account exposes them; the exact depth is not verifiable offline and is recorded from the authorized account (blocked_external until then).; open_and_related_records: Open disputes initiated before the window are included through the plan open-items phase; the disputed order is an optional link.; opening_state: Account balance snapshots (positions) are the opening state as of the poll time; they are never summed with ledger movements.; progress_accounting: loaded = disputes fetched; committed = distinct cases/postings with new entries; totals unknown.; readiness_rules: Dispute questions are ready for case status after a complete poll; booked debit/reversal answers are partial until the matching ledger postings are observed.; health_rules: healthy after a complete poll; degraded on missing read_shopify_payments_disputes.; history_expansion: Wider initiated_at window; status transitions already observed are duplicates.; data_handling: Only gids, amounts, types, statuses and timestamps; no bank account numbers (bankAccounts is not queried). |

</details>

| 2026-11-02T02:31:43Z / Claude Fable 5.1 (P4 re-run verifier) | shopify_payments.holds / implementation |   Re-records the implementation of row shopify_payments.holds on the committed revision df8cfbb5e41fb3da483270651823cf470aaf1a65 (P4 re-run verifier). The business mappings, adapters, reducers and query services are unchanged since the invalidated revision 482bdf5; the revision carries the engine health-latch fix in crates/projections/src/engine.rs and the MCP get_evidence description naming the explicit `restricted` status. Synthetic provider-shaped fixture only; live access remains a separate blocked event. Known limitations unchanged: All hold fields missing; capability unsupported.; mapping: Committed mapping artifact integrations/mappings/shopify_payments.holds.json (pinned GraphQL Admin API 2026-10, exact source paths, missing fields explicit, three mode declarations, nine import-readiness rules, event rules per family); validated by integrations/mappings/validate-mappings.py.; transport: packages/connectors/src/adapters/shopify-payments: no provider object exists for holds; client credentials or admin token (never logged), calculated-cost throttling, 429 Retry-After, 5xx backoff, expired cursors, account identity check (reauthorization_required on mismatch), gateway/public modes declared unsupported and refused.; postgres: apps/worker/src/sync.ts + packages/connectors/src/ingest.ts: deliveries dedupe boundary, journal_evidence, append_journal_entries(jsonb) as bb_worker under RLS, derived entries with input_entry_ids/derivation_rule, checkpoint after every COMMIT, conflicts as source.conflict_observed + review case, dead letters for rejected input.; rust: crates/domain reducers (effect-once per observation, duplicates/conflicts/late entries, corrections via supersedes_entry_id), crates/projections tail/checkpoint/snapshot/rebuild, bounded RAM with cold reads; verified by cargo test --workspace --release (golden checkpoints, adversarial variants, 1000-entry tail, corrupt snapshot rebuild).; query_mcp: crates/query intents + apps/api question/state routes + packages/mcp stdio bridge: identical normalized answers through API and MCP (SLICE-01 step 4/5), explicit partial/unknown/conflict with missing_inputs, committed/applied cursors and as_of cutoffs.; operations: capability_health (last successful check vs last new event, next check + reason, coverage), import_runs counters, worker dead letters, sanitized structured logs (no tokens/payloads), fences for backup/restore/upgrade; runbook in docs/connectors.md and docs/operations.md. | [integrations/mappings/shopify_payments.holds.json](integrations/mappings/shopify_payments.holds.json); df8cfbb5e41fb3da483270651823cf470aaf1a65 |

<a id="source-easybill"></a>
### easybill

**Pilot:** deferred. **Platform plan:** first_release. **Documentation:** reviewed.

**Input:** GET /documents and /documents/{id}; /document-payments; /post-boxes; document versions where available. Reconcile edited_at with overlap and stable page handling. Webhook notifications can trigger authenticated re-fetch; verify the documented signature scheme before trusting payloads.

**Prerequisites:** Authorized account/API credential and actual plan limits. Schema reviewed: REST v1, info.version 1.103.1, SHA256 6d532f96a7fe82fcbd556178f8bd2a11f364061690f67ccb932d3722ac2b1e86. Verify signs, correction linkage, historical version completeness and account-specific field population in fixtures and an authorized account.

<a id="source-dhl"></a>
### DHL Unified Tracking

**Pilot:** deferred. **Platform plan:** first_release. **Documentation:** reviewed.

**Input:** GET https://api-eu.dhl.com/track/shipments?trackingNumber=... for known shop shipment references; optional authorized push subscriptions with reconciliation. Back off and stop/slow polling terminal shipments according to the actual contract.

**Prerequisites:** Eligible tracking numbers, API subscription key, product coverage and quotas. Published Unified Tracking terms include deletion 30 days after delivery unless otherwise agreed. Resolve retention and permitted downstream use before live append-only ingestion; raw-data separation alone does not establish rights to retain derived tracking facts indefinitely.

## What must enter each data contract

Every capability above links here. These are required canonical inputs, not a claim that every provider supplies every field. Provider candidate paths are in [the source audit](specs/CONNECTOR-CAPABILITIES.md) and [the JSON crosswalk](specs/question-data-contracts.json). Each implemented row must add its own complete, verified field mapping.

**Shared route:** authorized receipt → immutable PostgreSQL journal and transactional outbox → committed cursor → deterministic Rust reducer → bounded RAM indexes and durable checkpoints → the same API/MCP query service. RAM is never updated ahead of the durable commit. Historical evidence stays addressable; cold data has an indexed fallback.

<a id="data-orders"></a>
### orders

- Required fields: `order_id`, `line_id`, `customer_id`, `item_id`, `variant_id`, `quantity`, `unit`, `currency`, `line_net_minor`, `line_tax_minor`, `line_gross_minor`, `discount_minor`, `shipping_minor`, `agreed_version`, `cancellation_state`, `updated_at`.
- Journal events: `order.accepted`, `order.revised`, `order.cancelled`.
- Applicable tracks: source, fact, commitment.
- PostgreSQL: Immutable typed payload and entity/evidence links in the canonical PostgreSQL journal; mutable current state only in rebuildable projections. Event family determines which listed tracks apply; never emit all tracks blindly.
- Rust / RAM: P_ORDER; see the projection definitions in the JSON crosswalk.
- Normalization: Preserve agreed and current versions; line removal/returns are not interchangeable. Use provider IDs scoped to account, never email/order number alone.
- Open proof: Line-price and partial-document matching needs explicit equivalence rules.
- Question coverage: order-02, order-03, order-04, delivery-02, delivery-03, delivery-04, invoice-02, invoice-03, collection-01, collection-02, reversal-03.

<a id="data-stock"></a>
### stock

- Required fields: `item_id`, `location_id`, `unit`, `observed_at`, `basis`, `physical`, `available`, `committed`, `reserved`, `damaged`, `safety_stock`, `quality_control`, `incoming`.
- Journal events: `inventory.snapshot`, `inventory.adjustment_observed`.
- Applicable tracks: source, fact.
- PostgreSQL: Immutable typed payload and entity/evidence links in the canonical PostgreSQL journal; mutable current state only in rebuildable projections. Event family determines which listed tracks apply; never emit all tracks blindly.
- Rust / RAM: P_STOCK; see the projection definitions in the JSON crosswalk.
- Normalization: One authoritative stock basis per item/location/cutoff. For disjoint states derive free = physical - committed - reserved - damaged - safety_stock - quality_control; compare to provider available. Never subtract the same commitment twice.
- Open proof: Snapshot differences alone do not prove the cause of a goods movement; incompatible snapshots produce a conflict.
- Question coverage: order-01, order-02.

<a id="data-allocations"></a>
### allocations

- Required fields: `allocation_id`, `order_id`, `line_id`, `item_id`, `location_id`, `quantity`, `unit`, `status`, `policy_version`, `evidence_id`.
- Journal events: `inventory.reserved`, `inventory.reservation_released`, `allocation.revised`.
- Applicable tracks: source, fact, reservation.
- PostgreSQL: Immutable typed payload and entity/evidence links in the canonical PostgreSQL journal; mutable current state only in rebuildable projections. Event family determines which listed tracks apply; never emit all tracks blindly.
- Rust / RAM: P_ORDER, P_STOCK; see the projection definitions in the JSON crosswalk.
- Normalization: An observed reservation and a derived allocation are distinct. Preserve provenance and releases; reconcile aggregates without double subtracting.
- Open proof: Fulfillment location assignment is not automatically a reservation. Implement explicit generic events or a declared allocation derivation.
- Question coverage: order-02, order-03, order-04, reversal-03.

<a id="data-supply"></a>
### supply

- Required fields: `promise_id`, `supplier_id`, `item_id`, `location_id`, `quantity`, `unit`, `promised_at`, `timezone`, `revision`, `allocation_links`.
- Journal events: `supply.commitment_recorded`, `supply.commitment_revised`.
- Applicable tracks: source, fact, commitment.
- PostgreSQL: Immutable typed payload and entity/evidence links in the canonical PostgreSQL journal; mutable current state only in rebuildable projections. Event family determines which listed tracks apply; never emit all tracks blindly.
- Rust / RAM: P_PROMISE; see the projection definitions in the JSON crosswalk.
- Normalization: A promise never increases physical stock; corrections append and supersede.
- Open proof: No supplier/PO connector is included at launch. Feed reviewed structured commitments through the generic API.
- Question coverage: order-01, order-04.

<a id="data-promises"></a>
### promises

- Required fields: `promise_id`, `order_id`, `line_id`, `promised_at`, `timezone`, `promise_kind`, `revision`, `evidence_id`.
- Journal events: `customer.commitment_recorded`, `customer.commitment_revised`.
- Applicable tracks: source, fact, commitment.
- PostgreSQL: Immutable typed payload and entity/evidence links in the canonical PostgreSQL journal; mutable current state only in rebuildable projections. Event family determines which listed tracks apply; never emit all tracks blindly.
- Rust / RAM: P_PROMISE; see the projection definitions in the JSON crosswalk.
- Normalization: Customer promise, fulfill-by date and carrier estimate are different assertions.
- Open proof: Must capture the actual communicated promise from a verified custom field/feed/reviewed communication; do not infer from shipping method.
- Question coverage: order-04, delivery-02, delivery-04.

<a id="data-fulfillments"></a>
### fulfillments

- Required fields: `shipment_id`, `order_id`, `line_id`, `quantity`, `unit`, `location_id`, `carrier`, `tracking_number`, `handover_at`, `delivered_at`, `state`.
- Journal events: `shipment.observed`, `shipment.dispatched`, `shipment.delivered`, `shipment.cancelled`.
- Applicable tracks: source, fact, movement, reservation.
- PostgreSQL: Immutable typed payload and entity/evidence links in the canonical PostgreSQL journal; mutable current state only in rebuildable projections. Event family determines which listed tracks apply; never emit all tracks blindly.
- Rust / RAM: P_SHIPMENT, P_ORDER; see the projection definitions in the JSON crosswalk.
- Normalization: Link actual line quantities to shipment milestones. Labels do not count as handover. Do not book stock movement twice from shop and carrier.
- Open proof: Multi-package line assignment may require packing/WMS data; unknown mapping limits line-level claims.
- Question coverage: order-03, delivery-01, delivery-02, delivery-03, delivery-04, invoice-02.

<a id="data-tracking"></a>
### tracking

- Required fields: `carrier_service`, `tracking_number`, `scan_identity`, `status_code`, `effective_time`, `timezone`, `forecast`, `observed_at`.
- Journal events: `carrier.scan_observed`, `carrier.forecast_observed`.
- Applicable tracks: source, fact.

## Verification boundary

Register checks prove structural consistency, immutable evidence references and recorded approval requirements. They do not prove that a person really ran a test or that an external account exposes every field. Inspect evidence and reproduce tests. Git branch protection / required checks must be configured separately by a repository administrator; this file alone cannot prevent an administrator from bypassing the workflow.
